| v38.157 | **Sesi KVM 4-CPU pertama di ISO v38.156 (AP akhirnya hidup) langsung nemu dua utang lama — satu bikin panic, satu bikin frame patah.** (1) **Panic SMP saat q3dm1 loading.** Driver ukur `/tmp/kvm_q3_measure.py` (KVM, `-smp 4`, `-m 512`, q3dm1, mouse digerak-gerakkan) boot sampai game jalan, lalu `[WATCHDOG] CPU 1 HUNG — no timer heartbeat for 300+ ticks (hard lockup, IF=0) — detected by CPU 2` dan `[PANIC] CPU 0 EIP=0x00156AEB / CPU 1 EIP=0x00100D80 CR3=0x0A122000 / [PANIC] halted`. EIP-nya dibongkar: `nm` bilang `0x00100D80` = `ata_write_sector_drive_io` tepat di instruksi `rep outsw` (ECX=0x90 dari 0x100 word, EDX=0x1F0, CR3 = address space proses game). Pelakunya `vfs_create_node → vfs_save`: mkdir `/tmp/q3home` milik game adalah node-create pertama di runtime, dan node table-nya **2048 sektor = 1 MB**, ditulis SATU perintah PIO single-sector per sektor, masing-masing dengan CACHE FLUSH sendiri, sementara `vfs_lock` dipegang dengan interrupt MATI — jadi core pemanggil benar-benar diam detik-an, dan watchdog-nya benar. Perbaikannya batched: `ata_write_sectors_drive` (DMA kalau ada), 16 transfer ≤128 sektor, satu flush per perintah; fallback loop lama kalau kmalloc gagal. Hasilnya: run KVM 4-CPU berikutnya 900 frame q3dm1 **nol watchdog, nol panic**, dan persistensi-nya dibuktikan dua boot berurutan di image yang sama: boot kedua `[VFS] loaded ok`, tanpa `load failed`/`rebuilding`/`save failed`. (2) **Keluhan "patah-patah" diserang dari sisi serial.** Jalur lama busy-wait bit THRE per byte dengan IRQ mati (dua VM exit per byte), jadi baris diagnosa ~550 byte per 20 frame membekukan frame yang menulisnya; sekarang penulis cuma append ke ring 16 KB dan yang mengurasnya tick timer SETIAP core (satu poll THRE lalu burst 14 byte ke FIFO; tanpa FIFO balik 1 byte), jalur panic flush ring dulu supaya urutan log tetap benar. Semua suite tetap baca bukti yang sama — nol file test yang diubah. **Yang diukur, bukan diklaim** (ISO `Q3_DEEP_DIAG` sekali pakai, KVM 4-CPU q3dm1, baris `glsplit`): `gl` total 3.0-3.8 ms/frame dari mana draw 2.8-3.8, `vm` 1.7-2.2, gun+status bar+overlay (`hud`) cuma 0.6-0.85, stats+histogram ~0.1, dan meter WM (`blit`+`draw`+`pass`) ~2.1. Jadi sisa `other_ms` 13-16 ms/frame itu **bukan** baris serial dan **bukan** HUD/gun: itu jalur present/composite (upscale 2x + gambar window ke back buffer + swap back→LFB). Pacer tetap di grid 50 ms (20 fps, `idle` 29-32 ms/frame) sampai jalur itu dikerjakan — lever berikutnya, dengan angkanya sebagai titik start. **Verifikasi:** build `MECTOV_Q3=1 Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` 0 warning, ISO 13.975.552 byte; suite `boot` PASS 12s (lebih cepat dari 16s), `q3vm` PASS 26s, `q3arena` PASS 87s, gate ACPI/SMP tetap mencetak 2 core; `third_party/q3a/` tidak disentuh. **Sesi pemain asli — TERBUKTI (log `serial_debug.log`, 4 Okt):** ~1070 frame q3dm1 (~53 detik), nol panic/watchdog; 26 laporan `[Q3HIT]` SEMUANYA `slack=0ms` (v38.156: ~22 ms di tiap baris), `fps=20` stabil, `look_s` 25-68/detik, `pkts_s` 75-344 — mouse look mengalir, "patah-patah" hilang. Belum dikerjakan: refactor jalur present (sisa composite 13-16 ms/frame). `OS_VERSION` 38.157 (150-156 sudah terpakai; 157 nomor bebas berikutnya). |
| v38.156 | **"kalo karakter di game nya gw gerakin pake mouse, selalu lag selalu patah patah, dan kalo cuman jalan doang pake wasd cuman dapet 16 fps" — dan jawabannya bukan di renderer: guest-nya cuma jalan di SATU CPU.** Sesi pemain 11:18 (`MECTOV_DISPLAY=sdl ./quake.sh`) dibedah lengkap, dan sisi input ternyata sudah bersih: `pkts_s=76` (sesi 10:56: `216`) dan `look_s=14-28` — paket PS/2 nyampe dan handler game benar-benar melihatnya, jadi "lag" yang tersisa adalah FRAME TIME. Angkanya: `sum_ms=5305..6568` per 100 frame (53-66 ms/frame, fps 15-16), `gl_ms≈2900` (raster 320x240 ~29 ms/frame), `other_ms≈1800` (~18 ms), `idle` 5-7, `vm` 2-4, `[Q3HIT]` interval 36..200 ms (median 50) dengan frame tunggal `gl=84/114/202 ms`, dan `slack≈22 ms` di TIAP baris Q3HIT — waktu yang tidak dipakai task game sama sekali. Pacer-nya menyerah di tengah jalan: `pacer: 25 ms -> 50 ms grid (sustained overrun)`, artinya loop ngunci di 20 fps, dan karena look dibaca sekali per frame, putaran mouse pelan jadi patah. **Akar masalahnya satu baris di boot:** `[ACPI] RSDT checksum failed!` lalu `[SMP] Only 1 CPU detected. SMP disabled.` — ada di SEMUA log `-m 512` (punya pemain maupun punya suite), sementara probe dengan RAM lebih kecil keluar `[SMP] AP startup complete. Active APs: 0x00000003` dari kernel yang identik. Penjelasan yang terukur: guard di `acpi.c` menolak setiap pointer tabel di atas 256MB (angka zaman identity map masih berhenti di situ), sedangkan SeaBIOS menaruh RSDT di strip reserved paling atas RAM — usable RAM habis di `0x1FFDF000`, RSDT-nya di `0x1FFE1D6F`. Jadi MADT tidak pernah di-parse, AP tidak pernah dibangunkan, dan seluruh desktop (task game, compositor, shell, tulisan serial yang busy-wait) berbagi satu core 1,7 GHz — itulah `slack` 22 ms dan spike `gl` sampai 200 ms. **Perbaikannya satu arah dan bukan tebakan:** bound-nya sekarang span yang benar-benar di-identity-map (`mem_identity_tables() * 2MB`, dibatasi plafon `PHYS_MAX_PAGES`), bukan ukuran RAM. Percobaan pertama memakai `phys_ram_bytes()` dan GAGAL di boot nyata — tabelnya 12 KB di atas RAM yang dihitung allocator — dan perbedaan 12 KB itu sekarang jadi komentar di kode, karena itu bedanya "mapping" dengan "RAM". Efek sampingnya gratis dan kelihatan di log: FADT dan DSDT ikut ter-parse lagi (`[ACPI] FADT: PM1a_CNT=0x00000604`), jadi jalur poweroff ACPI ikut hidup. Regresinya dijaga alat, bukan ingatan: `scripts/q3arena_test.py` (suite yang memang boot `-m 512`) sekarang meng-assert `[ACPI] Found MADT` + minimal 2 `Found CPU Core` dan menolak `RSDT checksum failed`/`Only 1 CPU detected` — dipilih dari baris yang dicetak BSP sebelum AP mana pun jalan, jadi tidak bisa dipecah oleh output AP yang interleaved. **Verifikasi:** build `MECTOV_Q3=1 Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` EXIT=0 tanpa warning, ISO 13.975.552 byte; boot KVM `-m 512 -smp 4` → `RSDT sig/len OK`, `Found MADT`, 4 `Found CPU Core`, 3× "is awake", login tercapai; `-m 128 -smp 2` → 2 core, login (tidak ada regresi di guest kecil); suite `boot` PASS 14s, `q3vm` PASS 23s, `q3arena` PASS 87s, dengan gate barunya mencetak `[OK] ACPI/SMP: 2 CPU cores online`. **Belum dibuktikan dan tidak diklaim:** sesi pemain berikutnya — `fps`, `slack`, dan interval `[Q3HIT]` di baris berikutnya yang menentukan apakah "tenang dan nyaman" itu tercapai; dan biaya raster ~29 ms/frame itu sendiri tetap ada (320x240 tidak disentuh), jadi targetnya frame yang rata, bukan 60 fps. Kalau setelah ini `other_ms`/`slack` masih tinggi, lever berikutnya sudah terdaftar di release notes berikutnya: baris diagnosa serial tiap 20 frame, composite ganda (game present sendiri + loop desktop), dan loop upscale 2x di `q3ref_blit`. `third_party/q3a/` tidak disentuh. `OS_VERSION` 38.156 (150-155 sudah terpakai sebagai tag sumber di tree ini). |

Satu bug kecil yang ketemu waktu menulis ini: `a_pkt_last` tidak ikut di-reset antar sesi, jadi jendela pertama peluncuran kedua bisa membaca delta sampah — sekarang ikut nol. `OS_VERSION` 38.149. |
| v38.148 | **"nengok pelan pake mouse patah patah gitu" — mouse look-nya nyampling di loop desktop, bukan di loop gambar.** Fix present v38.138 terbukti kerja di host sendiri: sesi 11:20 di ISO v38.138 load `total=24930ms` (dari 179,5 s di v38.137), `win_draw: now painting GAME (3D world)`, `pres_fps` 24-34, 8300 frame di 24-29 fps, **0 panic** — jadi "game nggak muncul" memang sudah selesai, dan keluhan barunya beda: pandangannya patah-patah waktu mouse digerakkan PELAN. Dari 28 jendela 100-frame di log sesi itu, tabelnya: `sum_ms` median **3522** lawan target 2500 (lewat 40%, tiap jendela, tanpa kecuali), `fps` median 27 (target 40 Hz **tidak pernah** kesampean), `gl_ms` median 2233 (89% budget cuma raster), dan `other_ms` median **665** — 6,7 ms/frame waktu yang dicuri dari task game, 19% dari frame. **Dua sebab, keduanya soal SIAPA yang menggerakkan apa.** (1) **Sudut pandang cuma bergerak kalau loop desktop berkenan.** Kamera render membaca arahnya live (`SHORT2ANGLE(delta_angles) + q3vm_cmd_yaw`, v38.143), dan `q3vm_cmd_yaw` cuma berubah di `q3arena_win_mouse` — yang satu-satunya pemanggilnya adalah idle loop kernel lewat `wm_capture_event`. Jadi laju aim tidak punya hubungan apa pun dengan laju render; gerakan mouse pelan datang menggerombol setiap kali compositor kebetulan dapat timeslice. `desktop_capture_pump()` (kernel.c + wm.h) sekarang dipanggil dari frame loop Q3 sendiri, sekali per frame, sebelum usercmd dibangun, jadi aim tidak pernah lebih tua dari frame yang menggambarnya. Dua pemanggil tidak bisa kehilangan packet: `mouse_take_delta()` atomik (cli, baca, clear), siapa pun yang jalan duluan mengambilnya. (2) **Desktop nge-composite seluruh layar 30x per detik di belakang game.** Selagi ada window yang memegang capture, `wm_draw_window()` (wm.c + wm.h) cuma menggambar rect window itu — latar, semua window lain dan taskbar tidak ada yang bergerak — sementara present penuh 1 Hz milik game sendiri tetap jalan, jadi jam taskbar dan readout sudut tetap hidup. **Ditambah instrumen yang selama ini tidak ada:** baris `perf` sekarang membawa `look_s`/`key_s` (event yang benar-benar sampai ke window game per detik), `comp_s`/`comp_ms` (biaya composite desktop selama capture dipegang, read+clear seperti `wm_q3_times`), dan `hm`/`kd`/`btn`/`idle_in` (empat gerbang rescue attract-mode, yang dulu tidak bisa dibedakan). **Yang ditolak atas bukti:** posisi mata dibuat live tanpa lerp seperti rencana awal — posisi harus tetap di rel tick (yang live cuma arah), dan `ps->origin` mentah justru 20 Hz bertingkat, jadi itu akan menambah judder, bukan mengurangi. **Cacat lain yang ketemu waktu audit:** baris pose dan pixels ternyata di grid 100-frame sementara catatan v38.145 di kode itu sendiri menspesifikasikan **20** ("this line STAYS on the 20-frame grid") — dan suite `q3arena` membuktikan catatannya benar: run 96 detik cuma sampai frame 100, jadi gate "level is being submitted" dapat SATU sample dan membaca `drawn=2` (sesi pemain sendiri di frame yang sama membaca `drawn=408` dan `653`). Guard luar blok itu jadi 20-frame (pose+pixels), guard 100-frame pindah ke dalam khusus baris perf dan diagnostik dev, dan tagihan serial-nya dibayar dengan menaikkan UART 38400 → 115200 (`init_serial`), karena `serial_putc_locked()` busy-wait dengan interrupt MATI — 260 us per byte di 38400. `scripts/q3arena_test.py` juga berhenti meng-hardcode `322x262`: geometry-nya sekarang diturunkan dari baris `rect=` di log dan yang di-assert adalah `content=320x240` (aturan tetap) plus hubungan `CONTENT*scale+2 x CONTENT*scale+TITLEBAR_H+2` — suite ini merah permanen di tiap ISO `Q3ARENA_SCALE=2`, yaitu build yang dipakai pemain, dan assertion yang selalu merah adalah titik buta. Sampah `a-q3_kernel.d`/`a-q3_vm.d` di root dibuang dan `*.d` masuk `.gitignore`. **Verifikasi:** build bersih EXIT=0, ISO 13.946.880 byte, `boot` PASS 16s, `q3vm` PASS 30s, dan **`q3arena` PASS 98s untuk pertama kali di ISO scale-2** (geometry `642x502 at (191,119) for 320x240 content (scale 2)`, peak 26 face, polaritas grid dikonfirmasi dari log suite: pose+pixels tiap 20 frame, perf tiap 100, `comp_s=5` artinya jalur per-window benar-benar terpakai). **Belum dibuktikan:** sesi pemain — `look_s` dan `fps` di baris `perf` berikutnya yang menentukan. `OS_VERSION` 38.148: nomornya lompat dari 138 karena 139-147 sudah dipakai tag perubahan di tree ini. |
| v38.138 | **"game ga muncul dan qemu freeze" — layarnya nyangkut di gambar loading, dan itu bug present gw sendiri.** Sesi 23:52 (ISO v38.136) memberi dua bukti yang akhirnya cocok: log bilang game jalan (`frame 380`, `drawn=799`, `warm=10587..16410`, tour jalan), sementara screenshot user memperlihatkan layar loading `loading 8s` dengan jam taskbar 23:54:17 — jadi yang salah bukan renderer-nya, tapi apa yang SAMPAI ke layar. Dua sebab, dua-duanya dari kerja gw sendiri. **(1) Damage rect bocor.** `mark_dirty()` (vga.c) cuma mencatat damage kalau render target-nya back buffer, sementara `swap_buffers()` me-reset rect yang baru saja disalin — jadi rect yang kelewat tidak akan pernah tersalin lagi dan layar mempertahankan isi sebelumnya. Pump v38.134 menggambar lewat jalur itu tanpa swap yang tak-syarat, dan begitulah layar loading tertinggal di kaca selama game jalan di belakangnya. Perbaikannya `desktop_present_now()` (kernel.c + wm.h): damage SELURUH layar dulu, baru composite + swap dari task pemanggilnya, sehingga swap-nya tidak bisa meleset; `desktop_pump()` memakainya, dan frame loop Q3 memanggilnya sekali per detik — game dijamin sampai ke kaca dalam satu detik walaupun jalur idle loop sedang bermasalah. **(2) Guest nge-composite 60 fps untuk layar yang berubah 1x per detik.** Hook progres tekstur memanggil `wm_invalidate()` PER TEKSTUR, dan invalidate itu yang menyalakan `needs_redraw` milik idle loop — artinya composite desktop penuh (gambar 1024x768, salin seluruh frame ke memori VGA, lalu QEMU mendorongnya ke window) sampai 60 kali per detik. Di host yang melaporkan ini, ISO yang sama butuh 179 detik untuk load (`vm=139390ms`) lawan **15,1 detik** di harness (`vm=3190ms`, `pump=20ms/5`) — dan 86 tekstur selesai jauh lebih cepat dari itu. Invalidate sekarang dibatasi 400 ms. **Ditambah dari laporan yang sama:** biaya pump diukur dan dilaporkan di baris load (`pump=Nms/C`, v38.137), rate pump 2,5 Hz -> 1 Hz, dan mode attract: tanpa tombol dan tanpa gerakan mouse selama 5 detik, tour demo mengambil kamera lagi (`tour resumed: no input for 5s`), karena layar yang berhenti berubah sudah lima kali dilaporkan sebagai freeze. **Verifikasi:** build bersih, ISO 13.955.072 byte, boot sampai `[K] login` tanpa panic, dan probe screendump framebuffer tiap 2 detik: game muncul 7,3 detik setelah perintah, view bergerak 24-38% setiap 2 detik dengan piksel hangat map — jadi "tampil di layar" sekarang diukur, bukan diklaim. Suite `boot`/`q3vm` belum dijalankan ulang untuk rilis ini karena image workspace masih dipegang sesi pemain. `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.138. |
| v38.136 | **"gw nunggu lumayan lama tapi game ga muncul dan qemu freeze" — self-driving tour-nya mati karena klik fokus, dan satu event mouse menyundul kamera ke lantai.** Laporan kelima, dan akhirnya penyebabnya ketemu dari log sesi 23:39 sendiri, bukan dari dugaan. Empat angka yang menutup kasusnya: **(1)** marker v38.135 `window raised again on input` kepicu di sekitar frame 110 — klik user memang sampai ke game dan jendelanya naik; **(2)** `pos=(928,1479,24)` TIDAK berubah dari frame 220 sampai 721, yaitu 500 frame diam di tempat; **(3)** `render totals: frames=721 faces=35601` = ~49 face per frame, sementara `pixels frame=20 warm=22596` dan `pixels frame=260 … warm=1 sky=0 bright=69` — view-nya kolaps jadi nyaris kosong; **(4)** kamera berakhir di `pitch=+82`, nunduk ke lantai. Sebabnya satu baris gate: tour v38.132 digantung pada `!a_input_seen`, dan `a_input_seen` diset oleh SETIAP event termasuk gerakan mouse — jadi klik user untuk memfokuskan jendela QEMU (yang mouse-nya di-capture game, sehingga sampai sebagai GERAKAN) mematikan tour beserta kedua detektor nyangkut-nya, dan event mouse PERTAMA setelah capture membawa seluruh travel pointer host, sehingga satu event itu memutar kamera 82 derajat. Hasil akhirnya kotak gelap statis 500 frame, ESC masih jalan, dan keluar dari game mengembalikan desktop — dari luar persis seperti "QEMU freeze yang lalu lancar sendiri". **Perbaikan, tiga lapis, semuanya di sisi kami: (1) `a_human_move`** — yang menghentikan tour sekarang hanya INTENT: tombol gerak (WASD/panah/SPACE/C/Ctrl) atau klik mouse; ESC (quit) dan F3 (panel perf) tetap perintah, bukan steering, dan gerakan mouse saja tidak lagi mengakhiri apa pun. **(2) `a_mouse_first`** — event gerakan pertama setelah capture dibuang (itu travel pointer, bukan aiming), plus setiap delta per-event di-clamp ±300 count: inilah bedanya "menangkap mouse" dengan "menyentak pandangan". **(3) Rescue** — kalau kontrol sudah diambil lalu pemain diam lebih dari 3 detik dan view-nya masih kolaps (`drawn * 4 < peak`), tour diambil alih lagi dan kamera diputar jalan, dengan marker `demo resumed: the camera had been left on a collapsed view`. Pemain yang berhenti di tempat dengan view normal tidak pernah disentuh: syaratnya bentuk kolaps DAN tiga detik tanpa tombol. Flag-nya juga di-reset tiap sesi, jadi `q3arena` kedua dalam satu boot mulai dari tour yang bersih. **Verifikasi:** build bersih (EXIT=0; warning yang tersisa cuma jobserver dan `-Ctarget-feature` milik Rust yang sudah lama), ISO 13.955.072 byte, kernel boot sampai `[K] login` tanpa panic, `boot` PASS 16s, `q3vm` PASS 30s — suite terakhir itu yang menjaga tour demo tetap berperilaku sama saat tidak ada input sama sekali (headless: `a_human_move` tetap 0) — dan `q3arena` lulus semua assertion fungsional (5 tekstur ter-decode, 29 face per frame, patch tessellated, mesh dari `maps/mectovtest.bsp`); satu-satunya FAIL adalah assertion ukuran window `322x262` yang ter-hardcode di harness karena ISO ini dibangun di `Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` (642x502), mismatch yang sama seperti v38.123. Yang belum bisa dibuktikan dari sini: jalur klik itu sendiri — tidak ada tangan yang bisa mengklik jendela dari harness — jadi pembuktiannya adalah sesi pemain, dengan dua marker yang harus muncul: `window raised again on input` (jendela naik sendiri) dan, kalau view-nya pernah kolaps, `demo resumed: the camera had been left on a collapsed view`. `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.136. |
| v38.135 | **"Di gw game nya ga muncul, masih stuck" — game-nya jalan, jendelanya yang di belakang, dan input yang jadi jalan keluarnya.** Laporan keempat, dan penyebabnya tidak ada di dalam guest sama sekali. Semua angka guest sehat: `load: engine=9310ms collision=220ms textures=29690ms vm=90920ms total=130140ms`, lalu frame 19.420 di 17 fps, `input_seen=1` muncul 124 kali (di sesi sebelumnya semuanya 0), nol panic. Yang salah adalah dua lapis penumpukan jendela. **(a) Di host:** `_NET_CLIENT_LIST_STACKING` menunjukkan jendela QEMU ketutupan `Freebuff Desktop` (1440x900 @51,114) dan satu window Brave fullscreen (1920x1053), sementara window aktif justru terminal xfce maximized (1920x1017) — dan jendela yang ketutupan tidak menerima satu pun event mouse atau keyboard, jadi guest-nya tetap hidup tanpa bisa dilihat. **(b) Di guest:** screendump host-side (PIL `ImageGrab` via X11; XFCE di sini tanpa compositor, jadi capture memang pixel yang benar-benar tampil) di rect yang log-nya sendiri sebut tempat window game (`rect=191,119 642x502`; guest cuma punya dua window: `Terminal (Ring 3)` id=0x1 dan `Quake III` id=0x2) ternyata berisi teks — warna dominan (17,17,27) plus teks abu-biru (186,194,222), ciri terminal, bukan view 3D. Sebabnya urutan yang tidak punya jalan kembali: klik user untuk memfokuskan jendela QEMU di host jatuh ke window terminal DI DALAM guest, dan WM guest mengangkatnya di atas window game, sementara mouse sudah di-capture game dan scancode sudah di-route ke game (v38.133) — jadi game-nya terus menerima input dan menggambar 17 fps ke window yang tidak terlihat, dan v38.131 hanya menaikkan window itu saat load dan saat frame pertama, tidak pernah lagi sesudahnya. **Perbaikan:** `q3arena_note_input()` (`third_party/q3mectov/q3_vm.c`) menyetel `a_raise_wanted` pada setiap key dan setiap event mouse (throttle 500 ms, dan dilewati selama boot window masih yang menggambar), lalu frame loop game — task yang sudah menaikkan window di frame pertama — yang memanggil `wm_raise()`. Permintaannya lewat flag, bukan panggilan langsung, karena handler key jalan di konteks compositor dan restack dari dalam paint bukan hal yang dijamin WM. Mulai rilis ini setiap gerakan mouse atau tombol yang masuk ke game otomatis mengembalikan window-nya ke depan, dengan marker sekali `window raised again on input (the terminal was in front)`. Plus satu alat host: `scripts/quake_focus.py` (ctypes libX11) yang mencari window QEMU lalu mengirim `_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW` + `_NET_WM_STATE_ABOVE` — `--no-pin`, `--off`, `--list` — karena kelas masalah yang sama (jendela VM terkubur di bawah window maximized) sudah dua kali jadi laporan, dan `quake.sh` mendorongnya sendiri enam kali di setengah menit pertama (tanpa pin, karena `run.sh` mem-`exec` QEMU di foreground). **Verifikasi:** build bersih 0 warning, ISO `MECTOV_Q3=1 Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` 13.950.976 byte, dan kernel boot sampai `[K] login` lewat QEMU read-only (`-snapshot` + `file.locking=off`, karena image workspace sedang dipegang QEMU pemain). Catatan penting soal suite: `boot` FAIL `kernel never reached login screen` di sini BUKAN kernel-nya — `scripts/check.py` memboot image workspace yang sedang dikunci QEMU pemain, jadi suite harus dijalankan setelah QEMU itu ditutup. `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.135. |
| v38.134 | **"Stuck aja sama kayak tadi" — layar loading yang diam satu fase penuh, dan kenapa invalidate saja tidak cukup.** Laporan ketiga soal hal yang sama, dan kali ini log-nya bersih: sesi 21:05 memang SUDAH masuk game (`load: engine=9550ms collision=220ms textures=17880ms vm=40070ms total=67720ms`, frame 300-5700, `win_draw paints: boot=332 game=2068`, nol panic) — yang bilang "stuck" adalah layarnya, bukan mesinnya. Diukur langsung: QEMU headless KVM dengan monitor socket, `screendump` tiap 4 detik selama sesi `q3arena q3dm1 nolimit`, lalu jumlah pixel yang berubah dihitung antar gambar. Hasilnya: t=0-12 s **0 pixel berubah** (fase engine), t≈16 s 24% berubah (fase tekstur — bar-nya jalan), t≈20-24 s 1% lalu **0%** (fase VM), t≈28 s 39% (= game muncul), lalu 15-39% per 4 s. Jadi jendela loading duduk di SATU gambar selama satu fase utuh — dan di host yang sibuk fase itu 40 detik. Sebabnya bukan menggambar, tapi siapa yang menggambar: jendela sudah memanggil `wm_invalidate()` sepanjang waktu, tapi compositor dijalankan di loop idle, dan kernel task yang tidak pernah nge-block (loader Q3: Com_Init ~10 s, lalu VM_Create/GAME_INIT) mengalahkan loop itu selama ia tetap runnable — `wm_tick_all()` yang jadi denyut layar loading pun tidak pernah dipanggil karena pemanggilnya adalah loop yang sama. (Ini juga menjelaskan dua gejala lama: bar yang cuma gerak di fase tekstur, dan `[quake]` progress dari shell yang tidak pernah muncul di log — task shell-nya juga kelaparan.) Perbaikannya dua lapis, seluruhnya di sisi kami: `desktop_pump()` (kernel.c, dideklarasikan di wm.h) mem-composite satu frame desktop DARI task pemanggilnya, dengan plafon 60 fps yang sama seperti loop idle, dan **memaksa** redraw — invalidate jendela memang tidak menyalakan flag `needs_redraw` milik loop, itu akar kedua. Lalu `q3arena_pump()` (q3_vm.c) memanggilnya dari tiga titik yang dimiliki loader: hook progress per-tekstur, **setiap potongan baca file engine** (`q3_fread` di q3_kernel.c — satu-satunya "event loop" yang dimiliki loader selama Com_Init) dan **setiap baris yang dicetak engine** (`q3_vprintf` — GAME_INIT banyak bicara dan sedikit membaca, jadi fase yang hanya di-hook di jalur baca tetap gelap sepanjang fasenya); setelah game jalan pump berhenti sendiri (`a_boot_ready`) dan jalur fullscreen tidak pernah disentuh. Sampling 1 detik membuktikan efeknya: gambar saat fase tekstur DAN fase VM berubah tiap sampel (1.196-3.621 px per detik = counter detik + bar + sapuan), bukan lagi 0 seperti sebelum patch. Dan ongkosnya diukur, bukan diasumsikan: versi pertama pump jalan ~8 Hz dan itu MEMBAYAR MAHAL — sesi berikutnya melaporkan `load: engine=8100ms collision=180ms textures=30040ms vm=73700ms total=112020ms`, artinya +65% dibandingkan 67.7 s tanpa pump (tekstur 17.9 → 30.0 s, VM 40.1 → 73.7 s), karena tiap pump adalah composite desktop penuh (desktop + dua jendela + swap). Throttle diturunkan ke 2,5 Hz — counter detik dan sapuan tetap terbaca bergerak, ongkosnya tinggal beberapa persen — dan hook cetak ditambahkan supaya fase yang sebelumnya gelap ikut hidup. Sisa yang masih jujur harus dicatat: fase compute murni di dalam VM_Create/GAME_INIT yang tidak membaca dan tidak mencetak apa pun tetap diam (itu yang membuat satu laporan terakhir masih berbunyi "stuck"), dan itu bukan lagi soal menggambar: loader menahan seluruh sistem (bahkan poll `[quake]` milik shell yang mestinya mencetak tiap ~5 detik tidak pernah muncul di log mana pun) — arah berikutnya adalah mengapa scheduler tidak pernah mem-preempt task ini. **Dan laporan terakhir ("masih stuck, cape banget") membongkar akar yang sebenarnya: bukan load, tapi presentasi.** Screenshot user memperlihatkan layar loading di `please wait - collision world`, `loading 10s`, jam taskbar **22:16** — sementara log sesi yang sama sudah jalan sampai frame 2000+ (`fps_win` 27-39). Guest-nya sudah in-game dan merender berapa menit pun, tapi frame-nya tidak pernah sampai ke layar: compositor + swap dijalankan oleh loop idle kernel, dan task Q3 tidak pernah menyerahkan CPU — `hlt` di limiter frame hanya menunggu tick timer, bukan memberi giliran ke task lain. Tiga bukti penunjuk: `win_draw paints: boot=368` mandek (nol paint lagi setelah fase engine), jam taskbar beku, dan poll `[quake]` milik shell nol kali sepanjang sesi (padahal prefix-nya jelas ada di cmd_q3arena.c). Fix: `task_sleep(1)` di frame loop saat jendela aktif (~3% dari budget 30 fps) plus satu lagi di `q3arena_pump()`. Di host tenang angka load tidak berubah (`total=15870ms` vs 15610 ms) dan layar berubah tiap detik sepanjang load maupun game (1-3,3k pixel/detik saat load, 24-39% saat main), tapi efek fix ini justru untuk host SIBUK — jadi konfirmasi akhirnya harus datang dari sesi user, bukan dari probe headless di host tenang. Yang tersisa dan dicatat apa adanya: masih ada jeda ~8 detik antara Enter dan paint pertama layar loading (driver belum menulis apa pun sebelum baris `args:`) — dua-duanya lever berikutnya, bukan lagi 40 detik beku. Sekalian: `run.sh` sekarang memperingatkan + jeda 5 detik sebelum rebuild tanpa `MECTOV_Q3=1` mengganti ISO yang ada, karena trap itu sudah dua kali terjadi (`./run.sh` polos rebuild bersih tanpa engine, dan satu-satunya gejala adalah `q3arena: engine not compiled in` di dalam OS) — peringatan itu diuji dengan harness yang mengekstrak blok aslinya dari `run.sh` dan men-stub QEMU: lima bentuk (KVM sehat, permission denied, akses ditolak, `MECTOV_KVM=0`, ISO tanpa-Q3) PASS. Verifikasi: `boot` PASS 13s, build 0 warning, ISO `MECTOV_Q3=1 Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` 13.950.976 byte. `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.134. |
| v38.133 | **"Loading-nya nggak bisa dibatalin, kursor hilang, terminal diam — makanya kerasa freeze": ESC sekarang membatalkan load, kursor tetap kelihatan sampai game benar-benar mulai, loading screen menghitung detik, terminal menceritakan tiap tahap — dan setiap tahap load diukur (Q3 phase 27).** Laporan "qemu nya freeze" diselidiki dari serial log sesi user (14:51-14:57) dan jawabannya bukan freeze: guest-nya SEHAT — `win_draw paints: boot=600`, lalu `game=524 -> 4124` paint, frame sampai 6480 di 29-32 fps, nol panic/fatal/OOM; QEMU berhenti 14:57:19 persis waktu user menutup jendelanya (log terputus di tengah baris, `[LOAD]` berhenti bersamaan). Yang bikin terasa freeze ada dua: **(a)** `q3arena_open()` memanggil `wm_capture_mouse()` SEBELUM 94 tekstur didecode (`mouse captured` di detik +48), jadi kursor hilang selama load, dan handler keyboard boot window (v38.131) MENGABAIKAN semua tombol termasuk ESC — dua sampai empat menit tanpa kursor dan tanpa satu pun tombol yang dijawab; **(b)** kontensi host: load average 8-9 di 8 core (dua proses agent ~87%, berebut QEMU), fps guest 2-4. **(1) ESC membatalkan load, di empat checkpoint.** `q3_drive()` mengecek `a_load_cancel` setelah `Com_Init`, setelah world load, setelah `q3arena_open()`, dan tepat sebelum frame loop; tiga yang pertama melepas sebisanya (window-only sebelum renderer ada, `q3arena_close()+q3bsp_free()` kalau renderer sudah hidup), yang terakhir melakukan teardown penuh seperti sesi berakhir sendiri (window, renderer, `GAME_SHUTDOWN`, `VM_Free`, park). Setiap pembatalan mencetak asal-usulnya: `[Q3ARENA] load cancelled: stage='collision world' at 7020ms (engine=1530ms collision=5470ms)`. **(2) Decode tekstur ikut menghormati pembatalan.** Hook progress berubah dari `void` ke `int (*)(int done, int total)` dan dipanggil SETIAP tekstur (dulu tiap 32) — return non-zero = berhenti; loop decode keluar sopan (`texture decode stopped early (cancel requested) at texture N`) dan jalur cleanup/summary di bawahnya tetap jalan, jadi renderer tetap konsisten. Gate 32-tekstur itu ternyata menunda jawaban ESC beberapa detik — alasan hook-nya sekarang per tekstur. **(3) Kursor dan keyboard diambil TEPAT saat game mulai, bukan sebelumnya.** Loading screen me-request scancode route saat dibuka (`load screen: ESC cancels (scancodes routed)`) dan hanya mengonsumsi satu tombol: ESC-down; tombol lain tetap diabaikan karena user masih mengetik di terminal. `wm_capture_mouse()` + re-request scancode terjadi di frame game pertama, berdampingan dengan `boot screen -> game (window raised)`, dan dilaporkan sebagai `[Q3ARENA] input: scancodes+mouse capture taken at game start (load 4360ms)`. Jadi selama load kursor tetap ada — bisa pindah jendela, bisa ESC, tidak ada tombol yang "hilang". **(4) Loading screen menghitung detik dan menulis hint.** Baris `loading Ns` dari `a_ms_since(a_load_t0)`, plus `ESC: cancel load, back to the desktop` (atau `cancelling at the next stage...` setelah ESC ditekan) — layar yang diam sekarang mengaku sedang apa dan berapa lama. **(5) Terminal ikut menceritakan load-nya, karena di sanalah perintahnya diketik.** API baru `q3arena_status()` (0 belum apa-apa, 1 loading `<stage> D/T (Ns)`, 2 `in the game: frame N, F fps`, 3 selesai) dipoll `cmd_q3arena` di task-nya sendiri: satu baris `[quake] ...` tiap ganti stage dan heartbeat tiap ~5 detik selama load, lalu berhenti saat game jalan (cap 5 menit supaya shell tidak tersandera). Window loading screen yang tertutup atau tertimpa terminal jadi bukan jalan buntu — progress-nya ada di dua tempat. **(6) Setiap tahap load diukur.** `[Q3ARENA] load: engine=...ms collision=...ms textures=...ms vm=...ms total=...ms` dicetak saat frame pertama, jadi "lama" berhenti jadi soal perasaan: di TCG (akses `/dev/kvm` belum tersedia selama verifikasi ini, jadi angka TCG ini yang tercatat) q3dm1 penuh adalah `engine=2480ms collision=10720ms textures=27800ms vm=2160ms total=43160ms`, sementara sesi 14:51 yang fps 2-4 itu adalah host-nya yang terkontensi, bukan guest-nya. **(7) Verifikasi.** Tiga suite di tree yang sama PASS: `q3arena` (119 s), `q3retail` (302 s), `q3snd` (240 s) — marker baru ada di serial log (`load screen: ESC cancels`, `input: ... taken at game start (load 4360ms)`, dan baris `load: engine=1910ms collision=130ms textures=1230ms vm=1090ms total=4360ms` di arena sintetis). Bukti baru `q3cancel_proof.py` (dua peluncuran satu boot, TCG): launch 1 ESC 1,5 s setelah loading screen → `load cancelled: stage='collision world' at 7020ms (engine=1530ms collision=5470ms)`, jendela load ditutup, desktop kembali, game tidak pernah dimulai; launch 2 jalan penuh sampai game dan keluar bersih dengan ESC → `[PASS] cancel + full run after it, two launches, one boot, no panic, no fatal`. Varian `--cancel-decode` (ESC di tengah decode, `texture decode stopped early (cancel requested) at texture 2`) juga PASS dengan bentuk teardown renderer (`releasing the renderer and the window`, desktop kembali) plus launch 2 penuh `engine=4560ms collision=14360ms textures=37200ms vm=3200ms total=59320ms` — satu boot, dua peluncuran, nol panic/fatal. Reliabilitas relaunch (v38.130) dicek ulang di KVM: dua `q3arena q3dm1` dalam satu sesi PASS, dan guest-nya sendiri melaporkan TSC host (`tsc_khz=1763`) — akses `/dev/kvm` dibalikin lewat keanggotaan grup `kvm`. Sesi normal di KVM pada host yang tenang: `load: engine=8840ms collision=420ms textures=3660ms vm=3620ms total=16540ms` (frame pertama 20,9 s setelah perintah diketik — lawan 43-81 s di TCG/host sibuk) dan permainannya mentok di cap 60 fps (`fps=60`, 5.500+ frame); angka fase tekstur 37-42 s di proof sebelumnya karena itu kontensi I/O host (42 s sibuk vs 3,7 s tenang), bukan batas CPU. **(8) Mulai yang cepat, dan log yang tidak hilang.** `./quake.sh --fast` (atau `MECTOV_QUAKE_FAST=1 ./quake.sh`) melewati `make clean_all` + `make` + `grub-mkrescue`: `run.sh` menghormati `MECTOV_SKIP_BUILD=1`, volume tetap di-seed, dan `mectov.iso` yang sudah ada langsung dipakai — jadi mulai main bukan rebuild penuh tiap kali (ISO yang dibuild tanpa `MECTOV_Q3=1` dideteksi dan diperingatkan, bukan diam-diam jalan tanpa engine). Dan `run.sh` berhenti men-`rm` log sesi sebelumnya: `serial_debug.log` yang lama dipindahkan ke `serial_debug.prev.log`, karena bukti sesi yang baru selesai tidak boleh hilang tepat saat orang relaunch — cara sesi 14:51 yang jadi dasar rilis ini sempat hilang. **(9) Satu bug ketemu, dan bukan dari rilis ini:** peluncuran engine KETIGA dalam satu sesi panic (`int_no=0x0E ... EIP=0x001ACE86` → `q3_memset+0x16`, di `Com_Init` ketiga). Reproduksinya memakai command headless `q3vm` tiga kali satu boot — tanpa window, tanpa cancel, tanpa renderer — jadi ini bug "fork engine ketiga per sesi" yang sudah ada sebelumnya; dipisah jadi milestone sendiri dan dicatat sebagai artifact `q3fork_probe.py`. Karena itu bukti cancel-nya dua peluncuran, bukan tiga. **(10) Fase baca — ongkos sebenarnya — dan cara ngeboronginya.** Diselidiki dari log sesi user (19:19 dan 19:30): gambar q3dm1 cuma **3,2 MB** (86 tekstur, 3,07 Mpx) tapi fase teksturnya 23 dan 46 detik, sementara di mesin tenang 3,7 detik — dan `[ATA] BMIDE DMA ready` membuktikan jalur disknya SUDAH DMA, jadi bukan soal PIO. Penyebabnya ongkos **per perintah**: `q3_fread` (`q3_kernel.c`) membaca file dalam potongan 1024 byte, dan `ext2_read_file_range` menerjemahkan tiap potongan jadi pembacaan **satu blok 4 KB = satu perintah ATA** (800 blok untuk 3,2 MB, plus pengulangan di dalam blok yang sama), sementara `ext2_read_file_data` melakukan hal yang sama untuk pembacaan whole-file (jalur boot). Latensi satu perintah itulah yang dikalikan beban host (~0,1 ms tenang → ~14 ms sibuk), jadi fase yang sama meledak 6-12x tanpa satu pun byte bertambah. Perbaikannya: `ext2_read_blocks_run()` + `ext2_read_lbn_run()` mengumpulkan blok fisisk yang berurutan jadi **satu perintah per sampai 64 KB** (`ata_batch_limit` sudah lama mengizinkan 128 sektor), dipakai oleh kedua pembaca file itu, dan jendela baca port 1 KB → 32 KB. Untuk set tekstur q3dm1 itu ~3.200 round-trip menjadi ~50. Terukur di mesin tenang, ISO dan argumen sama: `textures` 3.660 → **2.700 ms**, total 16.540 → **15.610 ms**, frame pertama 20,9 → 19,9 s. Yang tersisa di fase itu sekarang betul-betul decode JPEG (3,07 Mpx, ~0,88 µs/px) — dan itulah alasan cache tekstur hasil-decode baru masuk akal **setelah** bacaannya diborongikan: cache RGB565 6,1 MB / RGBA 12,3 MB justru lebih besar daripada 3,2 MB JPEG-nya, jadi tanpa patch ini cache cuma menambah byte lewat jalur yang lambat. Verifikasi di tree yang sama: `boot` PASS 16s, `iocache` PASS 34s, `lseekfile` PASS 35s, `bigread` PASS 93s, `q3vm` PASS 28s; `q3arena` hanya gagal di assertion `322x262` yang ter-hardcode karena ISO ini dibangun di scale 2 milik `quake.sh` (642x502) — mismatch harness yang sama seperti v38.123 — sementara semua assertion fungsionalnya (5 tekstur ter-decode dari volume, mesh, patch, surface) lulus. **(11) KVM yang gagal tidak boleh diam-diam jadi TCG.** Sesi 20:39 memainkan Q3 di TCG tanpa satu baris pun yang mengatakannya: `/dev/kvm` belum memberi akses pada detik itu (udev membuat ulang node-nya), QEMU mati < 8 detik dengan `Could not access KVM kernel module: Permission denied`, dan fallback `run.sh` — yang hanya mencari teks "KVM: entry failed" — menjalankan ulang semuanya di TCG dengan SMP 2. Yang terlihat cuma "Q3-nya kok 1 fps": view yang paling ringan pun (`drawn=277`, `vis=3452/6780`) ~1 s/frame (`gl_ms=21123` per 20 frame, `sum_ms=28450`) padahal raster yang sesungguhnya dikerjakan cuma `tot_kc=3386150` ≈ 2 ms pada 1,76 GHz — dan guest sendiri melaporkan `tsc_khz=0`, yaitu kalibrasi TSC gagal, yang di TCG memang tak mungkin dan cukup sebagai penanda TCG yang bisa dibaca dari log. `run.sh` sekarang: (i) mem-preflight `/dev/kvm` sebelum mencoba KVM dan, kalau tidak bisa dibaca/ditulis, langsung mengatakannya plus perintah `usermod -aG kvm` / `setfacl` alih-alih diam; (ii) mencetak 3 baris pertama stderr QEMU saat fallback, karena "Permission denied" dan "entry failed" itu dua masalah berbeda dengan obat berbeda; (iii) selalu mencetak `Akselerasi: KVM (-enable-kvm -cpu host, SMP 4)` atau `TCG (emulasi software, SMP n)` sebelum QEMU jalan. Diuji oleh harness yang mengekstrak blok ASLI dari run.sh (bukan salinan) dan men-stub QEMU: empat bentuk — akses ditolak, permission denied, KVM sehat, `MECTOV_KVM=0` — PASS. **Aturan tetap: resolusi render 320x240 tidak disentuh, `third_party/q3a/` tidak disentuh.** `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.133. |
| v38.131 | **"Game-nya di mana?!": jendela Quake III muncul DETIK PERTAMA sejak `q3arena` diketik — jadi loading screen bergerak (stage + progress bar tekstur) — dan naik ke depan sendiri pas game mulai.** Laporan user "udah nunggu lama, nggak muncul-muncul" diselidiki dengan repro visual (QEMU kedua + screendump per tahap lewat monitor socket): game-nya sendiri SEHAT — terbukti dari serial log (frame 1080+ di 24-25 fps, suara ngalir) dan screendump repro yang nunjukin q3dm1 render cantik (lava, viewmodel, HUD) — masalahnya murni urutan tampil: jendela baru dibuka SETELAH world load + decode 94 tekstur (2-4 menit di q3dm1), dan selama itu layar cuma nunjukin prompt terminal balik. Tiga perbaikan: **(1) `q3arena_boot_window_open()`** (q3_vm.c) membuka jendela ukuran final SEBELUM `q3vm_world_load()`, dan sampai frame game pertama `q3arena_win_draw` menggambar loading screen sendiri (font kernel 8x16, `font8x16_data`): judul, baris stage (`collision world` → `decoding textures` → `starting the game VM`), dan progress bar — persentase tekstur datang LIVE dari counter baru `q3w_progress_{stage,done,total}` yang di-update loop decode di `q3world_render.c`. **(2) `wm_tick_all()` akhirnya punya driver**: tick_fn ada di WM sejak v38.103 tapi TIDAK PERNAH dipanggil — main loop kernel sekarang memanggilnya tiap iterasi, dan tick boot window meng-invalidate jendela sehingga progress benar-benar beranimasi (guard `a_boot_ready` bikin biayanya nol pas game udah jalan). **(3) Auto-raise + input safety**: fase late-open me-`wm_raise` jendela (yang selama loading bisa keburu di bawah terminal yang diklik user), keyboard handler boot window MENGABAIKAN semua tombol (user masih ngetik di terminal — ESC yang ke-telan saat loading adalah bug yang sama seperti yang v38.130 fix), dan `a_boot_ready=1` + raise kedua terjadi tepat saat frame game pertama mau dirender (`boot screen -> game (window raised)` di log). **Plus `nolimit`**: argumen baru yang memainkan game sampai ESC — cap 10.800 frame itu untuk CI yang harus selesai sendiri, bukan buat pemain. Marker lama terjaga: baris `window id=0x... rect=...` cetak ULANG byte-identik saat game mulai (RECT_RE suite), plus baris baru `boot window id=` untuk fase awal; `q3arena_test` PASS penuh termasuk pixel assertions. `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.131. |
| v38.130 | **"Bikin q3arena bisa dijalanin dua kali dalam satu sesi — quit terus relaunch, tanpa reboot": a second `q3arena q3dm1` in one boot loads, plays and sounds like the first — the ESC→relaunch loop the user asked for, so a 2-minute map load happens once (Q3 phase 25).** Before this release the second launch died in id's own init: `Com_InitHunkMemory`'s `if (FS_LoadStack() != 0) Com_Error(ERR_FATAL, "Hunk initialization failed. File system load stack not zero")` — the quit path never runs FS_Restart/Com_Quit_f, so run 1's fourteen still-counted FS_ReadFile blocks fatalled run 2's Com_Init before it printed a single line. **(1) The engine reset.** `q3_drive()` (q3_vm.c) now does what FS_Restart would, before its own Com_Init: probe + report the load stack (`[Q3VM] relaunch: FS load stack was 14 on entry (a file stayed allocated through quit)` — visible, not papered over), `FS_Shutdown(qfalse)` (closes handles, frees the searchpath list, lets FS_Startup rebuild the world), `Hunk_ClearTempMemory()` + `Hunk_Clear()` (drops run 1's qvm, collision world and textures), and a new one-call shim `q3_reset_engine_state()` (q3_kernel.c) that clears files.c's static `fs_loadStack` through the one function the vendored tree gained (`FS_ResetLoadStack()` in files.c, appended after `FS_LoadStack` — `third_party/q3a/` keeps every existing line). The two memory zones are deliberately NOT cleared: the first write of the shim cleared them, the relaunch wedged inside CM_LoadMap with nothing on the wire, and the cause read plainly once pinned — id's own Com_InitZoneMemory re-callocs both zones wholesale right after (a fresh Z_ClearZone over fresh bytes), so the pre-clear was redundant at best, and a pointer into a cleared-and-rebuilt list is exactly the shape that wedges Z_TagMalloc's rover walk. **(2) The second bug the first green run exposed.** With the fatal gone, run 2 loaded the world, entered the game — and quit itself after ONE frame. The driver is a file of statics (`a_quit`, `a_frames`, the input channels, the perf meters, `a_win`), and nothing between runs cleared it: `a_quit` was still 1 from run 1's ESC (the loop breaks AFTER one frame), `a_frames` kept counting across runs (render totals read 103 vs 102 — the first visible clue), and the stale key array fed run 2 run 1's held keys. `q3arena_reset_driver_state()` (q3_vm.c) now resets every one of them before Com_Init — knobs back to their declared defaults (sort/hud/sky on, gaps 40/20), counters to zero, the key array emptied, gentity/ps offsets back to unknown — while deliberately KEEPING the one machine property, the measured TSC rate (`a_tsc_khz`), which is still true for the next run. **(3) The hunt, because the tooling is the story.** The wedge was pinned by a watcher that waited for the second `CM_LoadMap(...)` in the serial log and froze the VM through QEMU's monitor socket to dump every CPU's registers: the game task sat in syscall 9 (SYS_YIELD) with all cores halted — blocked, not spinning — which ruled the engine out and pointed at the driver; `myos.bin`'s symbol table resolved the EIPs straight from the monitor dump. The false PASS that first "confirmed" the fix (two proof instances racing one log and one QEMU) taught the harness an flock and run-scoped log offsets, and the final proof captures its own register freeze (a world-load wedge still writes `hang_capture.txt`) before it verdicts. **(4) Proof and regressions.** The two-run proof boots once and runs `q3arena q3dm1` twice in the session — run 1 to the frame loop + sound frame, clean ESC quit, run 2 to the same: PASS, with `world: CM_LoadMap` ×2, `entered the game` ×2, `snd frame=` ×2, `relaunch: driver state reset` ×2, and render totals 102 then 100 (`a_frames` started over); zero fatals, zero panics. Regressions on the shipping tree: q3arena PASS, q3snd PASS (3 boots), q3retail PASS. **The 320x240 render resolution is untouched; `third_party/q3a/` is untouched (one appended accessor).** `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.130. |
| v38.129 | **"Bikin suara Quake 3 keluar lewat SB16 — tembakan, langkah kaki, lompatan, jumppad": the arena has id's own SOUNDS now — the module's events are read by a tap that maps them exactly the way id's own client does, and the named WAVs load off the volume into a loader/mixer that pours a continuous mix into the kernel's SB16 driver (Q3 phase 24).** Two new files do the audio: `third_party/q3mectov/q3sound.c` (+ `.h`, 460 + 70 lines). A RIFF/WAVE parser walks the chunk list bounded by the buffer that was actually read (a lying header is tolerated, a chunk past the end is not), accepts what the demo pak actually ships — PCM tag 1, mono, 22050 Hz, 8 or 16 bits — and converts 16→u8 by `(v + 32768) >> 8` clamped, so the mixer sums unsigned bytes around 128. Registration is by CASE-FOLDED PATH (id's `trap_S_RegisterSound` spelling, `sound/weapons/machinegun/machgf1b.wav`), cached once, played on one of EIGHT channels — a free channel first, else the FURTHEST-ALONG one loses its slot, deterministic where a priority table would be guesswork — and `S_Update` mixes all live channels into one 2205-sample buffer (`acc * 100 / 127` master scale, clamped, `+128`) handed to `sb16_set_audio_buffer` every frame; the SB16's own DMA loop (32 KB, IRQ halves — untouched from DOOM's use of it) pours that continuously, so the mixer only ever refills. The tap in `q3_vm.c` is id's client code read back: `EV_FIRE_WEAPON` cycles `machgf1b..4b` the way `CG_FireWeapon` cycles its `flashSound[4]`, `EV_FOOTSTEP`/`EV_FOOTSTEP_METAL` take `parm & 3` through id's own `step1-4`/`clank1-4` tables, `EV_FALL_SHORT` → `land1`, `EV_JUMP` → `visor/jump1`, `EV_JUMP_PAD` → `jumppad.wav`, `EV_NOAMMO` → `noammo`, and `EV_GENERAL/GLOBAL_SOUND` resolve `eventParm` as the module's OWN `CS_SOUNDS` configstring index (captured at `G_SET_CONFIGSTRING`, so a map that names a sound plays it) — enum values spelled numerically because `bg_public.h` is not in this TU's include graph, the same discipline as v38.122's. The frame task consumes the module's `eventSequence` ring (`MAX_PS_EVENTS 2`, walked by sequence like id's client walks it) plus `externalEvent` (rotating `EV_EVENT_BITS` masked off, low 8 bits = the event, exactly how the retail client reads a snapshot), then pours the mix once per rendered frame. `q3arena_open` registers the SIXTEEN sounds one player hears on every map by name before the module's first frame and reports `[Q3SND] map sfx: 16 registered, 0 missing (16 named)`; `q3arena_close` stops the loop and frees the cache (one SB16 owner at a time, like DOOM's module). **(1) The counters are the evidence, and every path counts.** `q3snd_stats` reports registered / missing / played / skip / drop / active; a play with the mixer off is a SKIP (counted, silent), a play of a sound that never registered is a DROP, a play with the card present and the mixer on is a PLAY — so `nosound` is an honest A/B, not a dead branch: events are still tapped, counted per NAME in a 64-slot table the diag line reports its top of, and the `on=` field of the sampled line is the knob's state. The every-100th-frame line reads `[Q3ARENA] snd frame=100 on=1 reg=16 missing=1 played=34 skip=0 drop=0 active=2205 top=34:sound/player/footsteps/clank1.wav` (one line, one `reportf` — and two traps fixed because the first boots caught them: the kernel `vsnprintf` PARSES but IGNORES `ll`, so the line carries no 64-bit arg, and `reportf` appends no newline, which every earlier call site embeds and this one had to learn). **(2) The fixture, with no id bytes on the machine.** `build_q3pak.py` gained the sixteen WAVs the driver names — synthetic but in id's OWN formats (22050 Hz mono PCM tag 1; 16-bit for the weapon/footstep/land group, 8-bit for `world/jumppad.wav`, mirroring the real data each stands in for), half-amplitude sines whose frequency derives from the name — and `q3a_data.py --with-sounds` stages them through the REAL path (it already globbed the pak's `sound/*.wav`, so it needed nothing). `quake.sh` now stages with `--with-sounds` too (marker: `sound/world/jumppad.wav`), for the same reason v38.124/127 taught: a tree staged before this release has the map, the gun and the HUD but no sounds. **(3) CI, three boots, no speakers needed.** `scripts/q3snd_test.py` (`make check-q3snd`, registered in `check.py`, a step in the `quake3` job) attaches QEMU's SB16 to the `none` audio backend — a PRESENT device with a silent wire, so the DSP answers, the DMA loop runs and every counted play is a real hand-off — and boots three times: on (16 fixture WAVs load one line each with the fields the RIFF carried, `jump=4 fire=6` drives `played=34 skip=0 drop=0`, the tap's top sound is a fixture name played 34 times), `nosound` (the same line, `played=0 skip=34 drop=0` — loaded and tapped, counted, silent), and NO card at all (the machine every other suite boots: `[Q3SND] no SB16`, the mixer never claims ready, the same events are skipped not dropped, and the boot is green). One harness finding worth recording: the serial log's early lines are the only reliable place for boot-time evidence — the kernel log ring WRAPS after a few minutes of frames, and a suite that searched the whole file for the load lines would find them overwritten while the later frame lines survive; the suite reads the head for loads, the full text for counters. **(4) The real data, on the user's own q3dm1.** Staged with `--with-sounds` off the demo pak0 and booted with the same silent SB16: `[Q3SND] map sfx: 16 registered, 0 missing`, id's own files load as themselves (`snd 0 … machgf1b.wav rate=22050 bits=16 bytes=65652 ms=1487`), and with `jump=4 fire=6` the sampled line read `played=34 skip=0 drop=0 active=2205` — 34 of id's own sounds through id's own event chain into the kernel's own sound card. **(5) The rules that did not move.** **The 320x240 render resolution is untouched**; `third_party/q3a/` is untouched (CS_SOUNDS is spelled `(32 + 256)` guard-protected beside the v38.122 constants); the tap consumes events the module itself never reads, so no suite's player-state assertions move; `q3sound.c` compiles with ZERO warnings under a hand-run `-Wall -Wextra`, and `q3_vm.c` still carries exactly its three pre-existing ones (checked against `git show HEAD:…`). Regressions on the shipping tree: q3snd PASS, plus the eight Q3 suites (see the run at the bottom of this row's verification). `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.129. |
| v38.128 | **"Bikin langit q3dm1 digambar sebagai sky (skyparms dome + layer awan yang scroll), jadi nggak datar dan diam lagi": the level's sky is a SKY now — id's own cloud box, centred on the camera, filled by the shader's own layers at `skyparms`' cloud height and animating on the game clock (Q3 phase 23).** Until this release a `surfaceparm sky` surface was an ordinary wall, and v38.126's own "what is not here yet" list said so in one line: *"no multi-stage shader blending (`tcMod scroll/scale/turb`) — so q3dm1's lava and sky are static"*. What replaces it is `tr_sky.c`, ported into a new module `third_party/tinygl/q3sky.c` (+ `q3sky.h`; 690 + 127 lines): `RB_ClipSkyPolygons`/`ClipSkyPolygon`/`AddSkyPolygon` (the map's sky faces clipped against `sky_clip`'s six planes, `ON_EPSILON 0.1`, `MAX_CLIP_VERTS 64`, `vec_to_st`), `ClearSkyBox`, `MakeSkyVec`/`FillCloudBox`/`FillCloudySkySide` and `R_InitSkyTexCoords`. **(1) The map's sky surfaces are no longer drawn at all, and that is id's design, not a shortcut.** Their only job is telling the clip which of a box's six sides the camera can see sky in: `AddSkyPolygon` picks a side from a polygon's summed direction and grows that side's s/t bounds, so the filled box is the ANGULAR EXTENT of the sky the level exposes — a room with one sky ceiling paints sky exactly where the ceiling is, and nothing else. `FillCloudySkySide` then puts every generated point at `sv.viewParms.or.origin + point`, so the box is centred on the CAMERA: walking moves the sky with you and the clouds never get nearer, while `MakeSkyVec` sizes the box `zFar/1.75` (2341 units with this projection's 4096 far plane, its corners landing on the far plane). **(2) The coordinates are not a planar projection, and that is what makes it a dome rather than a painted wall.** For every one of the 6x9x9 grid points, `R_InitSkyTexCoords` traces the direction to a sphere of radius 4096 raised `cloudHeight` above the view (the quadratic solved for `p`), pushes the intersection up by `radiusWorld`, normalises it and takes the arc cosines of x and y — an angular coordinate pair, so a scroll moves the same amount in texture space everywhere on the dome while the on-screen step varies with the viewing angle. The table is a property of the CLOUD HEIGHT (built once, at load, with id's hard-coded 1024 zFar) and is reused for every frame at any zFar; the port brings its own `sqrtf`/`acos` (this link has no libm). **(3) Where it runs, and why it is the cheapest faithful thing here.** id draws sky fragments at the far end of the depth range (`qglDepthRange(1,1)`, so anything the level draws later wins); this rasterizer has no depth-range call, so the pass runs FIRST — before any opaque face, with the depth test off, into a depth buffer `q3ref_begin_frame` has just cleared. Every world pixel that follows overwrites it and a pixel the level does not paint stays sky, which is the same result for less work. Each stage of the shader is a layer, in file order: the base image opaque, a `blendFunc GL_ONE GL_ONE` stage with the only blend this rasterizer can express (see v38.125/v38.127). The tcMods are ioquake3's own arithmetic, applied in place in the order the script wrote them — `tcMod scroll` adds the FRACTIONAL part of `speed * shaderTime`, `tcMod scale` multiplies what is already there — with `tess.shaderTime` collapsed to the game clock because a sky shader has no `timeOffset`. Each side is one triangle strip per row of id's 8x8 grid (`SKY_SUBDIVISIONS 8`/`HALF 4`), covering exactly the quads `FillCloudySkySide`'s index list covers, and the bottom side is skipped (`i == 5`), as id does. **(4) The parser learned the keywords (`q3bsp.c`/`.h`).** `surfaceparm sky`; `skyparms <farbox> <cloudheight> <nearbox>` (a `-` in the middle = no cloud layer at all); and PER STAGE `map\|clampmap\|animMap`, `blendFunc GL_ONE GL_ONE`, `depthWrite` and up to two `tcMod scroll\|scale` in file order. A definition whose `skyparms` names no cloud height is not registered and keeps its faces on the old geometry path. **(5) A bug the first verification boot found, recorded because of how quietly it hid.** The image block was still inside `if (!captured …)`, so once a definition had its first image, every LATER stage's `map` operand was neither consumed nor recorded: q3dm1's additive cloud layer — and the second stage of any shader — was invisible to this release, and the fixture reported `stages=1 … layers=1/1` with an empty stage 1. Every `map` line is now consumed unconditionally and each stage's own first file image is stored beside the definition's (which also stops a filename from ever being read as a keyword). **And the second, found while writing this row: `nosort` would have deleted the sky.** The unsorted dev path does its culls inline in the emit loop, i.e. after the background pass, so the faces were collected and never drawn — `box=0` while the faces were still counted as drawn, invisible to every numeric assertion and visible only on screen. That path now collects the sky's faces in a pre-scan (same three tests) before the pass runs, and the inline loop only counts them; exercised by `q3arena mectovsky nohud nosort @-384,-384,114,45,20`, which reads `box=1 … tris=512`. **(6) The evidence line, the A/B, and the accounting.** Load prints one line per sky definition — `[Q3ARENA] sky: shader=textures/skies/tim_hell cloud=384 stages=2 farbox=0 tex0=29 img0=textures/skies/killsky_1.jpg tex1=30,add img1=textures/skies/killsky_2.jpg layers=2/2` — plus a run summary (`1 shader(s), 2 layer image(s) from disk, 0 missing`), and every hundredth frame prints `[Q3ARENA] sky frame=100 on=1 reg=1 cloud=384 stages=2 sides=1 tris=4 tris_run=400 box=1 box_run=100 fallback=0 fallback_run=0`. `nosky` turns the pass off (`a_sky`, joining `nosort`/`nohud`) and every sky face goes back to the geometry path — the A/B on one ISO. The world draw collects sky faces in its own cull pass (PVS/backface/frustum, unchanged), counts them as DRAWN (so the `drawn + culled + back == numFaces` invariant the suites read still holds — the face WAS drawn, by a box built from its directions), and reports the split as `box=`/`fallback=`. **(7) CI, with no id bytes on the machine.** `scripts/build_test_bsp.py` gained `build(sky_ceiling=True)`: a SECOND generated map (`maps/mectovsky.bsp`, in the synthetic pak only, so every other suite's arena is untouched) whose ceiling alone is painted `textures/mectovtest/sky`, defined the way q3dm1's own `textures/skies/tim_hell` is — `skyparms - 512 -`, an opaque base layer and an ADDITIVE cloud layer, each with its own `tcMod scroll`. Its two images are aperiodic mosaics on purpose (`build_q3pak.py`): a scroll that lands on a checker's period would put a working sky back where it started and turn "the layer moved" into a coin flip. `scripts/q3a_data.py` stages the map and its layers through the REAL path, and the new `scripts/q3sky_test.py` (`make check-q3sky`, registered in `check.py`, a CI step in the `quake3` job, whose budget is raised from 45 to 60 minutes for it) boots twice. Boot one, sky on: the load line must name both layers (`layers=2/2`, stage 1 `,add`), a box must be drawn every sampled frame (`cloud=512 stages=2 sides=3 tris=512`, run 51200, `box=1` face, `fallback=0`), the 74-surface mesh must be fully accounted for on all 8 sampled frames, and two screendumps 45 s apart must differ over the sky band and be PIXEL-IDENTICAL over the world band. Boot two, `nosky`: the definition still loads (1 shader, 2 layers — the knob is a draw-time switch), the box draws nothing, 1 face is back on the geometry path, the sky band holds no near-white pixel (only the additive layer can put the base layer's blue over 200 in every channel) and both dumps are identical in both bands. **Measured: PASS** — the band (186x77 px) held 1441 near-white pixels (the additive layer) and 2698 px of the base layer's blue, `3767` sky pixels changed between the dumps while `0` world pixels did, and with `nosky` nothing changed anywhere. The bands are where they are because the rest of the screen moves: a look-up boot on q3dm1 showed the diff between two dumps is the view model (the gun bobs while the module walks its own player, `x>=176, y>=140`) and the fps readout (content `y 5..15`), and neither falls inside a band. **(8) The real data, on the user's own q3dm1.** `[Q3ARENA] tex 27 textures/skies/tim_hell path=textures/skies/killsky_1.jpg` resolves, and the sky loads as `cloud=384 stages=2 farbox=0 … img0=…killsky_1.jpg tex1=30,add img1=…killsky_2.jpg layers=2/2` — id's own cloud height, both of id's layers off the volume (the script spells `.tga` and the pak ships `.jpg`; v38.125's resolution order is what finds them), the second stage recognised as the additive layer. At the map's own spawn (pinned pose `216,1328,50, yaw 0, pitch 25` — v38.119's `@` pins the eye and the view angles; the module keeps walking its player, which is why the frame line's `pos=`/`yaw=` are its own) the sky is a sliver of the map's sky direction: `sky frame=100 … cloud=384 stages=2 sides=1 tris=4 tris_run=400 box=1 fallback=0`, and between two screendumps 75 s apart the sky band changed in **155** of its 14880 pixels while the world band changed in **zero** of 5400. Not flat, and not still. **(9) What is deliberately not here, stated rather than implied.** id also draws two optional BOXES named by `skyparms` — a far one and a near one, six `env/*` images each, which the shader's own stages do not name. Neither is built: a map that asks for one reports `farbox=1` in the load line instead of quietly coming out with half a sky, and q3dm1's `skyparms - 384 -` asks for neither, so the map this release was measured on is exact. Still unported, unchanged by this release: `q3map_sun`/`RB_DrawSun` (there is no sun disc — that is the next milestone), the sky's own `q3map_surfacelight`, `tcMod turb/rotate/stretch/transform`, fog, decals and `env/*` reflections. **(10) The rules that did not move, and the suites.** **The 320x240 render resolution is untouched** — the box is drawn into the same finished ZBuffer every earlier row is about — and `third_party/q3a/` is untouched. `q3sky.c` compiles with ZERO warnings under a hand-run `-Wall -Wextra` (the Makefile builds the Q3 translation units with `-w`); `q3world_render.c` still carries exactly its four pre-existing ones (checked against `git show HEAD:…`). Regressions on the shipping tree: `q3vm` PASS 31s, `q3arena` PASS 103s, `q3vis` PASS 105s, `q3retail` PASS 103s (the shader-resolution suite, which the stage-image fix touches directly), `q3heavy` PASS 90s, `q3jump` PASS 134s, `q3viewmodel` PASS 132s, `q3hud` PASS 221s — with one harness finding worth recording: `q3hud` FAILED when run eighth in a back-to-back batch and PASSED alone, because its frame-20 histogram comparison is a comparison of the CAMERA, and the camera's deterministic walk is fed by the tick catch-up, i.e. by wall clock. It is the walk, not the sky: nothing this release draws is in that histogram. `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.128. |
| v38.127 | **"Tambahkan HUD Quake 3 asli (health/armor/ammo/score) dari gambar gfx/2d di pak0, pakai nilai playerState": the status bar retail draws is on screen — ammo, health, armor and the FFA score boxes, every one of them a picture out of the user's own pak0, every one of them the game module's own `playerState` (Q3 phase 22).** What was ported is `CG_DrawStatusBar()` from id's cgame/cg_draw.c, in a new module `third_party/tinygl/q3hud.c` (+ `.h`): the ammo field with the machinegun's ammo icon after it, the health field, the armor field with `icons/iconr_yellow` after it, and `CG_DrawScores`' FFA branch at the bottom right. **(1) The 15 pictures, and every one of them was already on this machine.** `gfx/2d/numbers/{zero..nine,minus}_32b.tga` (11 fields, 32x32), `gfx/2d/bigchars.tga` (the 256x256 16x16-cell charset the score box uses), `gfx/2d/select.tga` (the "this one is yours" overlay), `icons/icona_machinegun.tga` and `icons/iconr_yellow.tga` — cg_main.c:834-838 registers the number fields in exactly that order (`sb_nums[]`), :871 `selectShader`, :964 `armorIcon`, :1875 the charset, and each weapon's ammo icon comes from bg_itemlist (`icons/icona_*`). `scripts/q3a_data.py --with-hud` stages them (22 names, so any weapon the icon name table knows is covered) and `quake.sh` now asks for them, so the user's own `pak0.pk0`-staged tree grew from 136 to 158 files. Each load is a line — `[Q3ARENA] hud img 0 gfx/2d/numbers/zero_32b path=gfx/2d/numbers/zero_32b.tga size=32x32 bytes=4140`, `[Q3ARENA] hud img 11 gfx/2d/bigchars path=gfx/2d/bigchars.tga size=256x256 bytes=262188` — and the summary `[Q3ARENA] hud: 15 image(s), 0 missing (id's own status bar art)`; a volume without the art draws nothing and says so once. **(2) The layout is id's, quoted at the point of use rather than re-derived.** cg_local.h:67-72's `CHAR_WIDTH 32`, `CHAR_HEIGHT 48`, `ICON_SIZE 48`, `TEXT_ICON_SPACE 4`, `STAT_MINUS 10` and q_shared.h's `BIGCHAR 16x16`; `CG_DrawStatusBar`'s row `y = 432`, ammo at `x = 0` with width 3 and its icon at `CHAR_WIDTH*3 + TEXT_ICON_SPACE`, health at `x = 185`, armor at `x = 370` with its icon after it. All of it is authored in Q3's virtual 640x480 and scaled by `content/640`, `content/480` — id's `CG_AdjustFrom640` — so at the port's 320x240 a number arrives at 16x24 px, the size the digits have in the user's own screenshots. `CG_DrawField`'s clamp switch, its `Com_sprintf("%i")`, its `l = min(strlen, width)` and the `x += 2 + CHAR_WIDTH*(width - l)` that right-aligns a short number inside the field are id's arithmetic, transcribed; `CG_DrawChar`'s 16x16 grid in that 256x256 atlas is `(ch>>4, ch&15)` cells, and `CG_DrawStringExt`'s `%2i` with a space per cell is what right-aligns the score box's number. The colour table is id's four entries — amber `(1.0, 0.69, 0.0)`, red `(1.0, 0.2, 0.2)`, grey `(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)` for `weaponstate == WEAPON_FIRING && weaponTime > 100`, white for health over 100 — plus the `(cg.time >> 8) & 1` amber/red flash below 26 health, and the armor field AND its icon are both inside id's `if (value > 0)`, which is why a 0-armor screenshot shows neither. The FFA score box is `y = 480 - ICON_SIZE` then `y -= BIGCHAR_HEIGHT + 8`, `strlen("%2i") * BIGCHAR_WIDTH + 8` wide and flush to x = 640, filled with a 33%-alpha red quad and overlaid with `gfx/2d/select` when the score in it is yours. **(3) It is not drawn through GL, and the reason is this fork's rasterizer, not a preference.** Every HUD picture is 32-bit RGBA with real alpha and id blends it with `GL_SRC_ALPHA`; `third_party/tinygl/src/misc.c`'s `TGL_BLEND_FUNC` implements `GL_ONE`/`GL_ONE` and the `ONE_MINUS_*` cases and a `GL_SRC_ALPHA` request falls through to `GL_ONE`, so the blend id asks for cannot be expressed at all. The HUD therefore composites itself into the finished `0x00RRGGBB` ZBuffer with `dst*(1-a) + src*a` by hand — the same trick v38.110's perf panel and v38.126's readout already use — which also means the world pass pays nothing for it and the frame histogram the other suites assert on (read before the HUD is composited) is still exactly the 3D pass. The pictures come out of their own 32-bit TGA decoder, which keeps the alpha the world's decoder throws away (type 2 and type 10, either origin, BGRA), and the blit is a box average of the source each destination pixel covers: a status-bar digit arrives at 16x24 out of a 32x32 source, which is what id's GPU gets from a linear filter, done by hand because there is no sampler state here to set. **(4) The A/B knob and the evidence line.** `q3arena q3dm1 nohud` turns the bar off (`nohud` joins `nosort` as a keyword, `a_hud`), and every hundredth frame prints `[Q3ARENA] hud frame=300 on=1 health=100 armor=0 ammo=100 score=0 weapon=2 images=15 missing=0 draws=2700 digits=1800` — the values it was handed and what it made of them, so "the art loaded" and "a bar was drawn" stay two different numbers. **(5) What is not here, stated rather than implied by a screenshot:** the head slot (`CG_DrawStatusBarHead` needs `models/players/<model>/head_*.md3`, and there is no player model on this volume), the 3D ammo and armor models (id's 2D icon path is drawn instead), the team background (`CG_DrawTeamBackground` returns unless `PERS_TEAM` is red or blue, and this port's player is TEAM_FREE), `CG_DrawPowerups`, the frags/chat lines, and the crosshair is still the port's own rather than `cg_drawCrosshair`. **(6) CI can test this with no id bytes on the machine, because the numbers are decodable off the screen.** `scripts/build_q3pak.py` writes a synthetic picture set in id's own formats whose number fields are flat colours where the RED CHANNEL IS THE DIGIT, plus a bigchars atlas, a select overlay and both icons; `scripts/q3a_data.py --with-hud` stages it through the REAL staging path; the new `scripts/q3hud_test.py` (`make check-q3hud`) overlays it on a freshly formatted volume and, after the boot, reads the six number cells of the ammo and health fields back off a screendump AT ID'S OWN X POSITIONS (`field_cells()` reimplements `x += 2 + CHAR_WIDTH*(width-l)`) and requires them to decode to the module's own numbers (100 and 100), the ammo icon at id's icon offset, the armor icon NOWHERE, `gfx/2d/select` inside the score box, and — in the `nohud` boot — not a single number, icon or score-box pixel anywhere, with the frame-20 histogram identical between the two runs. **(7) Three harness findings worth recording, because each one made a working port look broken.** (a) `debugfs`'s `mkdir` does NOT create intermediate directories and **exits 0 when it fails**, so the suite's first two runs wrote the whole picture set into thin air and the port reported `hud: 0 image(s), 15 missing` with every file "staged"; the overlay now creates every ancestor explicitly. (b) `q3a_data.py --out` is the staging ROOT — the game directory it writes is `<out>/baseq3`, which `seed_ext2.sh` mirrors onto the volume's `/baseq3` — and the first version of the test treated `<out>` as if it were that directory, leaving the art one level above where id's own FS looks for `gfx/2d/...`. The suite also now formats and seeds its volumes through `scripts/q3_images.py` like every other Q3 suite, instead of inheriting whatever the previous run left. (c) **The health field is a moving target, and id's own code is why:** a player spawns at `ps.stats[STAT_MAX_HEALTH] + 25` (g_client.c:1191) and g_active.c counts that point a second back down to `STAT_MAX_HEALTH` while it is over, so the driver's samples read 115, 105, 100, 100 — the bar is being handed a live `playerState`, four times over, and the screendump waits for the FLOOR (100, where id's rule puts the field back in amber after the white "over max health" colour) so the pixels can be compared against a number that has stopped moving. The ramp itself is asserted, not assumed. **(8) The real data, on the user's own q3dm1.** The demo pak staged with `--with-hud` (158 files) and `q3arena q3dm1`: fifteen `hud img` lines naming id's own files, and the screendump decoded against id's own number art (each cell matched to the art's alpha, IoU) reads the ammo field as **100** and the health field as **121** — while the last frame marker that session printed is `frame=40 t=4000`, i.e. 125 minus 4 seconds of the module's own countdown, which is 121. Armor is 0, so the armor field carries zero amber pixels and no `iconr_yellow` anywhere; health is over 100, so the field is drawn in id's white rather than amber; an ASCII map of the bar's band shows the `100` glyphs at virtual x 2..98 and the machinegun's ammo icon at x 100..148. **And at the user's own scale.** The bar is composited into the 320x240 frame before either present path upscales it, so at `Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` — `quake.sh`'s default, the window in their screenshots — the window content is 640x480, which is exactly the virtual screen the layout is authored in: a number field arrives at 32x48 px, id's own `CHAR_WIDTH`/`CHAR_HEIGHT`, the size retail draws them at, and the same alpha-IoU decode of that dump reads the ammo field 100 and the health field 100, with the driver's samples at that scale reading `115, 105, 100` on the health column and `armor=0`, `images=15 missing=0`. **(9) Suites and the rules that did not move.** `q3hud` PASS (264s, re-run 256s on the tree that also lets `q3ref_shutdown()` free the bar's decoded pictures), and re-run green with the rest: `q3arena` PASS 100s, `q3vis` PASS 94s, `q3retail` PASS 80s, `q3vm` PASS 30s, `q3heavy` PASS 86s, `q3viewmodel` PASS 133s, `q3jump` PASS 122s. **The 320x240 render resolution is untouched** — the HUD is composited into the same finished frame every earlier row is about, and the F3 panel's cost accounting is unchanged (`o_hud` absorbs the bar the way it absorbs the gun, and the bar is ~1,700 blended pixels of an 76,800-pixel frame). `third_party/q3a/` is untouched, and `q3hud.c` compiles clean under `-Wall -Wextra` (checked by hand: the Makefile builds the Q3 translation units with `-w`). CI gained the step (`quake3`: the two boots above, `--timeout 600`, and that job's budget raised from 35 to 45 minutes for it). `OS_VERSION` bumped to 38.127. |
| v38.126 | **"yang gw kotakin merah mending apus aja, ganti menjadi keliatan berapa fps nya aja pojok kanan atas dan tulisan nya jangan jelek gitu nggak enak diliat" (Q3 phase 21).** The user boxed the in-game perf panel in a screenshot and asked for the opposite of a HUD: one fps number, top-right, and text that does not look bad. **(1) The red box is gone from the default view.** What it held was ~124x56 px of 25%-blended panel over a 320x240 frame: six numbers in four rows, a 5 px cost bar with one 5 px cell per 2 ms of frame budget, drawn straight into the ZBuffer by `q3ref_draw_perf_overlay()`. Every one of those pixels was a pixel of the 3D picture the user is playing inside. The panel is not deleted — it is this port's only instrument for "where does the frame go", and a release that cannot show it cannot be profiled — it is now behind **F3** (`q3ref_set_perf_detail()`, default off, logged as `[Q3ARENA] perf detail on (F3)`): scancode 0x3D reaches the game window untouched because with raw-scancode focus the kernel consumes only F12 (GDB stub), Ctrl+C/Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Alt+L, so the toggle needed no kernel change; its log line uses `write_serial_string` rather than the frame task's `reportf`, because the key handler runs in the compositor's context and `reportf`'s scratch buffer belongs to the render task (the same reason the neighbouring ESC branch writes that way). **(2) The text is drawn AFTER the upscale, and that is the fix for "tulisan nya jangan jelek".** At `Q3ARENA_SCALE=2` — the user's own window — the old hand-drawn 4x8 glyphs reached the screen as 8x16 blocks of doubled pixels, which is exactly what the screenshot shows. The readout is now painted into whatever buffer the frame is being presented from (`q3ref_blit()`'s window content buffer, `q3ref_present_fullscreen()`'s back buffer) with the kernel's own anti-aliased 8x16 DejaVu-Sans-Mono face (`src/include/font_aa.h`, the same data `draw_char_px()` blends with), so at the window's own resolution it is a real 8x16 glyph per character: grey `fps` label, white value, 4 px from the image corner, one near-black offset copy underneath as a shadow (the q3dm1 lava field is as bright as white text) and no panel at all. **The 320x240 render resolution is untouched** — it is the 3D picture, and the standing rule is that it never goes down; the readout has no reason to obey it. The readout also has no reason to obey the window: it is anchored inside the image rect, which both present paths repaint from scratch every frame, so a shorter value ("fps 9" after "fps 120") cannot leave stale glyph pixels and nothing needs clearing. A side effect worth naming: with the panel off by default the ZBuffer carries no HUD at all, so the frame histogram — the suites' pixel evidence — is the 3D pass and nothing else. **(3) The number itself was wrong for the question it is asked to answer, and the user's own log is the proof.** `perf frame=4900` (their session, 13:34, windowed q3dm1) reads `fps=20` — a RUN average since the loop started — while that window's own phases sum to `vm_ms=47 gl_ms=689 blit_ms=4 draw_ms=17 wm_ms=51 other_ms=160` = 45 ms/frame (~22 fps) and the parked view (`sum_ms=398/20`) runs at ~50. An average that lags the game by minutes is not "berapa fps nya". The HUD now shows the last sampled window's own fps (20 frames over that window's wall milliseconds, the same clock the limiter and the server tick feed use); the average stays in the `perf` line's `fps=` field, which is what the suites parse, and the dev-only `glsplit` line gained `fps_win=` and `detail=` so a log states both numbers side by side. **(4) Verified by pixels, in both present paths and both scales, on the REAL ISO with the user's own q3dm1 volume staged** (KVM, headless, screenshots scanned per pixel): windowed (642x502 window, content 640x480 at 192,140) the white digits occupy (806,146)-(826,154) and the grey label 56 px to their left, with `teal=0` in the whole content area — the panel's `C_GL` teal (0x00E5C0) is the panel signature on a red/orange map, and it is absent; after F3 the same scan reads `teal=2184 amber=536`, i.e. the panel is back. Fullscreen (`q3arena q3dm1 fullscreen`, 1024x768, text at 2x because the screen is >2x the render) the digits occupy (986,12)-(1013,29) with `teal=0`, and F3 brings `teal=5075`. An ASCII render of the readout region (one character per screen pixel) shows "fps" then two digits with their shadow, which is how the glyphs were checked without a human squinting at a screenshot. ESC ends the windowed session cleanly both times. **(5) The second question — "apa yang belum di quake 3 arena ke mectov os" — with the answer this row is written from:** no audio of any kind (no `S_StartSound`/`S_RegisterSound`/`trap_S_`: no shot, step, jump, hit, ambience or music); no entity besides the local `playerState` (no item pickups, no weapons on the floor, no opponents or bots, no rockets/explosions/gibs, no triggers, teleporters or jump pads — there is no `R_AddEntity` path at all); no brush submodels (`CM_InlineModel` is absent, so doors, lifts, moving platforms, crushers and secret walls do not exist); no mapmodels (the md3 torches, lion head, gratelamp); no multi-stage shader blending (`tcMod scroll/scale/turb` — so q3dm1's lava and sky are static — no `animMap` past frame 0, no `env/*` skybox, no fog, no decals); only the machinegun view model and only its bind pose (no weapon switching, no hands, no model animation; the muzzle flash is this port's procedural additive one, from v38.125); no Q3 HUD (health/ammo/armor/score/frags/chat — **v38.127 ported `CG_DrawStatusBar`, so health, ammo, armor and the FFA score box are on screen as of that release; frags/chat and the crosshair are what is left of this item**) and the crosshair is the port's own rather than `cg_drawCrosshair`; no menu, console, scoreboard or free key binding; no client-side prediction, so the pose trails the input by roughly a tick; and no networking at all. What IS there — id's own `qagame.qvm`, Pmove, the BSP world with PVS and lightmaps, the real pak0 textures through the shader-script resolver, the machinegun view model, additive fire and the 320x240 render — is what the earlier rows are about. **(6) The performance pass went in as an INSTRUMENT, and it answered the question the last one could not: where does a heavy view's GL phase actually go — and the honest answer turned out to be "the raster's own fill, which this release cannot make cheaper, so it did not pretend to".** The v38.125 session left a 40x contradiction on the table: `gl_ms=34.5` per frame against ~15 ms inside TinyGL's cycle counters, and the counters themselves read as if the machine's TSC rate were known (the `kc` fields are `cycles>>20`, a ratio until somebody says what a millisecond costs). **(a) The TSC rate is now measured, not assumed.** `a_tsc_calibrate()` arms a baseline on the first frame and takes the rate from the first frame at least 200 ms later (capped at a 400 ms window so the cycle count stays inside 32 bits — this link has no `__udivdi3`; a window that overran re-arms instead of inventing a rate, because on TCG the virtual TSC jumps in irregular leaps and a rate measured across a stall is fiction). It reads **1.738-1.774 GHz** on this host, which is what an invariant 1.6 GHz i5 TSC plus clock slop should read — so the old numbers were right in shape and wrong in units, and the new `[Q3ARENA] glms` line reports the same split in **microseconds per frame** (with `tsc_khz=0` and `-1` where the TSC is not trustworthy, rather than a number that only looks measured). **(b) Two new counters, and one of them caught its own author.** A shaded-fragment counter (`tgl_n_frag`, incremented in the four DT1 textured raster variants) plus the world pass's own non-raster split (`q3w_cpu_stats`: `prep` = PVS marking + cull loop + distance sort, `total` = the whole `q3w_draw`) turn `fill` and "everything else in the pass" into numbers. The first run of the new split printed `emit_kc=-2000`, a NEGATIVE leftover — because `tgl_cyc_vert` accumulates over the window and the **view model draws through the same context**, so `total - prep - vert` subtracted the gun's transform as if the world had spent it.
